  Georgium Podiebradium

Publisher: 1592 printing ("ex vetusto quodam codice recusa")
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Published 1592; public domain by date. Archive.org
  item metadata carries no explicit possible-copyright-status tag but
  shows access-restricted-item: None (freely downloadable), checked
  directly against the item's own metadata, 2026-09-25, this

  publication date.

Provenance note: Body text unaltered including its own OCR errors
  (Latin). A major contemporary hostile source on the Hussite wars,
  by a future Pope who observed events at close range. No PD
  English translation was located this pass. Vendored 2026-09-25 as
  part of a post-Step0-Revision-2 PD source-acquisition pass. Per
  this project's own INTAKE.md rule, this Latin text is a second
  witness, never primary evidence for a Representative.
